# Splitting Sales Representatives: every case in the QA lead's pattern (26 Oct 2026)

Rule 117 fourteenth amendment (QA lead, 26 Oct 2026: "Yes, permanent, all suites"). **DRAFT: nothing written to TestRail, Jira, Slack or Google; no commit.** Generated by `pattern-format-2026-10-26/make_report.py`.

## Summary

- Cases: 180 (174 library cases, 6 design-recheck cases in `DR-PATTERN.json`). Every case in `testrail-id-map.csv` is covered.
- C665915 (S1-01) is the QA lead's own model: its fields are byte-identical to before (it was already in the pattern).
- Byte identity of everything below the results (exact quotes with their From: lines, References from Design, held / Blocked notes, AUTOMATION line) and of title, refs and every other field: **180 of 180 identical**.
- No fact lost: 2100 facts (amounts, numbers, dates, quoted labels, product terms) taken from the old preconditions (state lines), steps and results; **0 not found** in the new text.
- Values that appeared only on a "For example:" line and were dropped as examples: 995 (placeholders and example records such as ZZAUTOTEST names, S2-4000x work order numbers and the example location; they stay in the setup file for Claude sessions).
- Stricter check: every fact of an old result is in the new results of the same case, and every fact of an old step in the new steps: **0 misses**.
- Gate: `python3 authoring/build.py` prints 0 PROBLEM lines (pattern gate replaces the Scenario gate; every other gate kept).

## How the pattern was applied

- Preconditions: written by hand per case (`authoring/pattern_cases.py`), in setup order: Sign in, the staff members, the customer, the records and their state, anything done after (a switch turned off, an account deactivated), the design pictures, then where to open it. Placeholders became plain names (sales rep A, staff member D, customer B, the first work order, the part sale, user B); values a result depends on stay as plain facts (amounts, hours, dates, settings); example-only values were dropped. The Blocked line is last ("If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done"."), except on C665915, which keeps the QA lead's own words.
- Steps: the case's own steps, with placeholders turned into the same plain names (one step reworded: S2-13 step 1, "the existing the second work order" → "the existing second work order").
- Results: the case's own words split into one bullet per fact at sentence and semicolon boundaries, and by hand where a sentence joined two facts with "and" or a comma. A name inside quoted screen text (a log entry, a printed row) is shown as [sales rep A's name]; "{Sales-rep-B} (Inactive)" became "sales rep B's name followed by "(Inactive)"".
- Setup facts added where the old preconditions left them implied (no behaviour added): S1-02 (sales rep A's last name sorts before sales rep B's, which the old example names gave), S5-19 (staff member C has the switch on when picked), and the order of events where a switch is turned off or an account deactivated after the person was picked (S1-07, S1-08, S2-07, S2-07b, S2-07c, S4-03b, S4-04c, S4-15, S4-15b, S4-20b, S4-26, S6-01, S6-02).
- Design cases: the browser-window and design-pictures line became "Set the browser window to …" and "Open the design pictures … attached to this case beside the browser window at 100% (actual size)." with the picture names, boards and the sample-data sentence word for word.
- Below the blank paragraph after the results nothing changed (checked byte for byte below).

## Second pass (26 Oct 2026): preconditions relate to the steps and results, and every case stands alone

QA lead: the preconditions "must make sense and relate to the steps and expected results below"; "if one test case fails that does not block the other test case." Two permanent gates were added to `authoring/build.py` (both end with 0 PROBLEM lines):

- **Relevance gate** (`ssr_lib.relevance_problems`): every record a precondition names (sales rep A-D, staff member A-D, customer A-C, user A/B, role A/B, location A/B, the first/second/third work order, part sale, location or customer) must be named in the steps or expected results, or be the record the steps start on (the precondition that opens it); otherwise the bullet naming it must say why it is needed with a "so ..." clause. Any other record is a PROBLEM.
- **Independence gate** (`ssr_lib.independence_problems`): no case key or C-id in the tester text; and every record a step or a precondition changes (picks or changes a rep, adds or removes a field, invoices, reverses, splits, moves lines, reopens, saves or imports a customer, turns a switch off, deactivates, reactivates or renames a staff member, a staff record that no longer exists) is described as made "for this test" in the preconditions, unless a step creates it. C665915 only reads and is kept as it is.

Facts dropped because their record was unused: 2
- S8-05: label `Sales Representative` (the two sales reps' "Sales Representative" switch: the reps were removed, no step or result uses them)
- S8-05: term `Sales Representative` (same bullet as above)

## Per case, old vs new

### S1-01 — C665915 (https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/665915)

Title: Sales Representative field appears below Service Advisor when unassigned

Below the results byte-identical: quotes yes, design block yes, notes yes, AUTOMATION line yes; other fields unchanged. Facts checked 4, not found 0.

**Old preconditions**

  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Use a customer with no Sales Representative assigned.
  - Have an open, uninvoiced work order for that customer, with no Sales Representative assigned, one approved labor line and one part.
  - Use a desktop browser at least 1024 pixels wide.

**New preconditions**

  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Use a customer with no Sales Representative assigned.
  - Have an open, uninvoiced work order for that customer, with no Sales Representative assigned, one approved labor line and one part.
  - Use a desktop browser at least 1024 pixels wide.

**Old steps**

  1. Open the work order and look at its details card without hovering over the Sales Representative field.

**New steps**

  1. Open the work order and look at its details card without hovering over the Sales Representative field.

**Old results**

  - Step 1:
    - The fields appear in this order: Lead Technician → Service Advisor → Sales Representative.
    - Sales Representative appears directly below Service Advisor, with its label on the first line and Unassigned on the second.
    - Its text style, size, weight and left alignment match the other two fields.
    - No border, dropdown arrow or button appears around or beside the field.

**New results**

  - Step 1:
    - The fields appear in this order: Lead Technician → Service Advisor → Sales Representative.
    - Sales Representative appears directly below Service Advisor, with its label on the first line and Unassigned on the second.
    - Its text style, size, weight and left alignment match the other two fields.
    - No border, dropdown arrow or button appears around or beside the field.

### S1-02 — C665916 (https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/665916)

Title: Sales Representative list starts with Unassigned, then reps by first name

Below the results byte-identical: quotes yes, design block yes, notes yes, AUTOMATION line yes; other fields unchanged. Facts checked 5, not found 0.

**Old preconditions**

  - Scenario: Your shop has three sales representatives (two share a first name), a staff member who is not a representative, and an open work order with nobody on it.
  - You are signed in as a user with the "Admin" role at {Location-A}.  
    For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919.
  - Three active staff members at {Location-A} have their "Sales Representative" switch on: {Sales-rep-A}, {Sales-rep-B} and {Sales-rep-C}. {Sales-rep-A} and {Sales-rep-B} share a first name; {Sales-rep-C} has a later first name.  
    For example: {Sales-rep-A} is ZZAUTOTEST Ann Able, {Sales-rep-B} is ZZAUTOTEST Ann Brook and {Sales-rep-C} is ZZAUTOTEST Ben Able (first name "ZZAUTOTEST Ann" or "ZZAUTOTEST Ben").
  - Staff member {Staff-member-D} is active at {Location-A} with the "Sales Representative" switch off.  
    For example: {Staff-member-D} is ZZAUTOTEST Dan Noswitch.
  - Customer {Customer-A} has no sales representative.  
    For example: {Customer-A} is ZZAUTOTEST SSR Fleet.
  - Work order {Work-order-1} for {Customer-A} is open and not invoiced; it has one approved labor line and one part, and its card shows one "Sales Representative" field reading "Unassigned".  
    For example: {Work-order-1} is S2-40001.
  - Starting screen: {Work-order-1} is open in a desktop browser at least 1024 px wide.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

**New preconditions**

  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Have three active staff members at the test location with their "Sales Representative" switch on: sales rep A, sales rep B and sales rep C. Sales rep A and sales rep B share a first name and sales rep A's last name comes first in the alphabet; sales rep C has a later first name.
  - Have staff member D active at the test location with the "Sales Representative" switch off.
  - Use a customer with no Sales Representative assigned.
  - Have an open, uninvoiced work order for that customer, with one approved labor line, one part and one "Sales Representative" field reading "Unassigned".
  - Open the work order in a desktop browser at least 1024 pixels wide.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

**Old steps**

  1. Click the Sales Representative field.

**New steps**

  1. Click the Sales Representative field.

**Old results**

  - Step 1:
    - a dropdown opens under the field. Reading it from top to bottom: the first option is "Unassigned" and it is checked. The sales representatives follow, sorted by first name, then last name, A to Z, so {Sales-rep-A} comes before {Sales-rep-B}, and {Sales-rep-B} before {Sales-rep-C}. There is no search box, and {Staff-member-D} is not listed.

**New results**

  - Step 1:
    - A dropdown opens under the field.
    - Reading it from top to bottom: the first option is "Unassigned".
    - "Unassigned" is checked.
    - The sales representatives follow, sorted by first name, then last name, A to Z, so sales rep A comes before sales rep B, and sales rep B before sales rep C.
    - There is no search box.
    - Staff member D is not listed.

### S1-03 — C665917 (https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/665917)

Title: Picking a sales representative saves at once and closes the list

Below the results byte-identical: quotes yes, design block yes, notes yes, AUTOMATION line yes; other fields unchanged. Facts checked 5, not found 0.

**Old preconditions**

  - Scenario: Your shop has two sales representatives and an open work order with nobody on it, and you give the work order a representative.
  - You are signed in as a user with the "Admin" role at {Location-A}.  
    For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919.
  - Two active staff members at {Location-A} have their "Sales Representative" switch on: {Sales-rep-A} and {Sales-rep-B}.  
    For example: {Sales-rep-A} is ZZAUTOTEST Ann Able and {Sales-rep-B} is ZZAUTOTEST Ben Able.
  - Customer {Customer-A} has no sales representative.  
    For example: {Customer-A} is ZZAUTOTEST SSR Fleet.
  - Work order {Work-order-1} for {Customer-A} is open and not invoiced; it has one approved labor line and one part, and its card shows one "Sales Representative" field reading "Unassigned".  
    For example: {Work-order-1} is S2-40001.
  - Starting screen: {Work-order-1} is open in a desktop browser at least 1024 px wide.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

**New preconditions**

  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Have an active staff member at the test location with the "Sales Representative" switch on: sales rep A.
  - Use a customer with no Sales Representative assigned.
  - Create an open, uninvoiced work order for this test for that customer, with one approved labor line, one part and one "Sales Representative" field reading "Unassigned".
  - Open the work order in a desktop browser at least 1024 pixels wide.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

**Old steps**

  1. Click the Sales Representative field.
  2. Click {Sales-rep-A} in the dropdown.
  3. Reload the page.
  4. Click the Sales Representative field again.

**New steps**

  1. Click the Sales Representative field.
  2. Click sales rep A in the dropdown.
  3. Reload the page.
  4. Click the Sales Representative field again.

**Old results**

  - Step 1:
    - the dropdown opens.
  - Step 2:
    - the dropdown closes and the field reads {Sales-rep-A}. There is no Save button near the field; the change is already saved.
  - Step 3:
    - after the reload the field still reads {Sales-rep-A}.
  - Step 4:
    - {Sales-rep-A} is the checked option.

**New results**

  - Step 1:
    - The dropdown opens.
  - Step 2:
    - The dropdown closes.
    - The field reads sales rep A.
    - There is no Save button near the field.
    - The change is already saved.
  - Step 3:
    - After the reload the field still reads sales rep A.
  - Step 4:
    - Sales rep A is the checked option.

### S1-04 — C665918 (https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/665918)

Title: Picking Unassigned clears the Sales Representative field

Below the results byte-identical: quotes yes, design block yes, notes yes, AUTOMATION line yes; other fields unchanged. Facts checked 5, not found 0.

**Old preconditions**

  - Scenario: An open work order has a sales representative, and you take them off so nobody is credited.

**Old preconditions**

    For example: {Sales-rep-A} is ZZAUTOTEST Ann Able, {Sales-rep-B} is ZZAUTOTEST Ben Able and {Sales-rep-C} is ZZAUTOTEST Cara Able.

    For example: {Customer-A} is ZZAUTOTEST SSR Fleet.
  - Work order {Work-order-1} for {Customer-A} is open and not invoiced; it has one approved labor line and one part, and its card shows "Sales Representative - Labor" reading {Sales-rep-A} and "Sales Representative - Parts" reading {Sales-rep-B}; it has two approved lines.  
    For example: {Work-order-1} is S2-40001.

  - Use a customer whose one Sales Representative is sales rep C.

  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

  2. Open the new work order.

**New results**

    - A new work order is created.
  - Step 2:

    - Neither field shows the customer's sales rep C.

### S4-07 — C665946 (https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/665946)

Below the results byte-identical: quotes yes, design block yes, notes yes, AUTOMATION line yes; other fields unchanged. Facts checked 11, not found 0.

**Old preconditions**

  - You are signed in as a user with the "Admin" role at {Location-A}.  
    For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919.
  - Two active staff members at {Location-A} have their "Sales Representative" switch on: {Sales-rep-A} and {Sales-rep-B}.  
    For example: {Sales-rep-A} is ZZAUTOTEST Ann Able and {Sales-rep-B} is ZZAUTOTEST Ben Able.
  - Customer {Customer-A} has {Sales-rep-A} as its one sales representative.  
    For example: {Customer-A} is ZZAUTOTEST SSR Fleet.
  - Starting screen: {Customer-A}'s Edit Customer window is open in a desktop browser at least 1024 px wide.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

**New preconditions**

  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Have two active staff members at the test location with their "Sales Representative" switch on: sales rep A and sales rep B.
  - Create a customer for this test whose one Sales Representative is sales rep A.
  - Open the customer's Edit Customer window in a desktop browser at least 1024 pixels wide.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

**Old steps**

  1. Click the +.
  2. Click "Sales Representative - Service Parts".
  3. Change Service Parts to {Sales-rep-B}.
  4. Close the window without saving (Cancel or the window's close button).
  5. Open Edit Customer again.

**New steps**

  2. Click "Sales Representative - Service Parts".
  3. Change Service Parts to sales rep B.

  5. Open Edit Customer again.

  - Step 2:
    - Service Labor and Service Parts show, both {Sales-rep-A}.
  - Step 3:
    - Service Parts reads {Sales-rep-B}.
  - Step 4:
    - the window closes; the page still shows one "Sales Representative" {Sales-rep-A}.
  - Step 5:
    - the window shows one "Sales Representative" field reading {Sales-rep-A}; nothing was saved.

**New results**

  - Step 1:
    - The menu opens.
  - Step 2:
    - Service Labor and Service Parts show, both reading sales rep A.
  - Step 3:
    - Service Parts reads sales rep B.
  - Step 4:
    - The window closes.
    - The page still shows one "Sales Representative" sales rep A.
  - Step 5:
    - The window shows one "Sales Representative" field reading sales rep A.
    - Nothing was saved.

  - Scenario: A work order was invoiced today with nobody on Labor and a person on Parts, while its customer has one representative.

  - Two active staff members at {Location-A} have their "Sales Representative" switch on: {Sales-rep-A} and {Sales-rep-B}.  
    For example: {Sales-rep-A} is ZZAUTOTEST Ann Able and {Sales-rep-B} is ZZAUTOTEST Ben Able.

    For example: {Customer-A} is ZZAUTOTEST SSR Fleet.

    For example: {Work-order-1} is S2-40001.

**New preconditions**

  - Have two active staff members at the test location with their "Sales Representative" switch on: sales rep A and sales rep B.
  - Use a customer whose one Sales Representative is sales rep B.

  2. Expand {Sales-rep-A}.

**New steps**

  2. Expand sales rep A.

  - Step 2:
    - the part is credited to {Sales-rep-A} on a "Parts" row. {Sales-rep-B} is not listed in the report: nothing is credited to {Sales-rep-B}.

**New results**

### S4-18 — C665957 (https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/665957)

Title: Deactivating a rep clears their customer fields and removes Part Sales

Below the results byte-identical: quotes yes, design block yes, notes yes, AUTOMATION line yes; other fields unchanged. Facts checked 18, not found 0.

**Old preconditions**

  - Scenario: A sales representative is on two customers, on one of them only for part sales, and you deactivate that person's staff account.
  - You are signed in as a user with the "Admin" role at {Location-A}.  
    For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919.
  - Three active staff members at {Location-A} have their "Sales Representative" switch on: {Sales-rep-A}, {Sales-rep-B} and {Sales-rep-C}.  
    For example: {Sales-rep-A} is ZZAUTOTEST Ann Able, {Sales-rep-B} is ZZAUTOTEST Ben Able and {Sales-rep-C} is ZZAUTOTEST Cara Able.
  - Customer {Customer-A} shows "Sales Representative - Service Labor" {Sales-rep-A}, "Sales Representative - Service Parts" {Sales-rep-B} and "Sales Representative - Part Sales" {Sales-rep-C}; customer {Customer-B} has {Sales-rep-C} as its one representative.  
    For example: {Customer-A} is ZZAUTOTEST SSR Fleet and {Customer-B} is ZZAUTOTEST SSR Other.
  - Starting screen: Settings > Staff is open in a desktop browser at least 1024 px wide.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

**New preconditions**

  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Create three active staff members for this test at the test location with their "Sales Representative" switch on: sales rep A, sales rep B and sales rep C.
  - Create customer A for this test, with "Sales Representative - Service Labor" set to sales rep A, "Sales Representative - Service Parts" set to sales rep B and "Sales Representative - Part Sales" set to sales rep C.
  - Create customer B for this test, whose one Sales Representative is sales rep C.
  - Open Settings > Staff in a desktop browser at least 1024 pixels wide.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

**Old steps**

  1. Start deactivating {Sales-rep-C}'s account and read the Deactivate window.
  2. Confirm the deactivation.
  3. Open {Customer-A}'s page.
  4. Open {Customer-B}'s page.

**New steps**

  1. Start deactivating sales rep C's account and read the Deactivate window.
  2. Confirm the deactivation.
  3. Open customer A's page.
  4. Open customer B's page.

**Old results**

  - Step 1:
    - the Deactivate window reads "They will be removed as sales representative from 2 customers." ({Customer-A} once, {Customer-B} once); the old line "Their customer assignments will stay where they are." is not shown.
  - Step 2:
    - {Sales-rep-C} is deactivated.
  - Step 3:
    - {Customer-A} shows Service Labor {Sales-rep-A} and Service Parts {Sales-rep-B}; the Part Sales field is gone, so new part sales follow Service Parts.
  - Step 4:
    - {Customer-B} shows "Sales Representative" reading "Unassigned".

**New results**

  - Step 1:
    - The Deactivate window reads "They will be removed as sales representative from 2 customers." (customer A once, customer B once).
    - The old line "Their customer assignments will stay where they are." is not shown.
  - Step 2:
    - Sales rep C is deactivated.
  - Step 3:
    - Customer A shows Service Labor sales rep A and Service Parts sales rep B.
    - The Part Sales field is gone, so new part sales follow Service Parts.
  - Step 4:
    - Customer B shows "Sales Representative" reading "Unassigned".

  - Work order {Work-order-1} for {Customer-A} has one labor line of 1.00 hour at $100.00 ($100.00), one part sold at $200.00 and a whole-invoice discount of $1.00; work order {Work-order-2} for {Customer-A} has one labor line of 1.00 hour at $100.00, one part sold at $100.00 and a whole-invoice discount of $0.01. On both, Labor reads {Sales-rep-A} and Parts reads {Sales-rep-B}, and both were invoiced today.

  - Step 2:

    - Adjustments on the first work order's row reads ($0.67) (the exact share 200 ÷ 300 × 1.00 = 0.666… rounded to the nearest cent), so together ($1.00).

Below the results byte-identical: quotes yes, design block yes, notes yes, AUTOMATION line yes; other fields unchanged. Facts checked 19, not found 0.

**Old preconditions**

  - Scenario: A work order with different Labor and Parts representatives and a part of known cost was invoiced today.
  - You are signed in as a user with the "Admin" role at {Location-A}.  
    For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919.
  - Two active staff members at {Location-A} have their "Sales Representative" switch on: {Sales-rep-A} and {Sales-rep-B}.  
    For example: {Sales-rep-A} is ZZAUTOTEST Ann Able and {Sales-rep-B} is ZZAUTOTEST Ben Able.
  - Customer {Customer-A} has no sales representative.  
    For example: {Customer-A} is ZZAUTOTEST SSR Fleet.
  - {Location-A}'s Shop Supplies Charge is 0%, and nothing else is invoiced at {Location-A} today.
  - Work order {Work-order-1} for {Customer-A} exists; it has one labor line of 2.00 hours at $100.00 an hour and one part with cost $30.00 sold at $50.00; Labor reads {Sales-rep-A} and Parts reads {Sales-rep-B}; it was invoiced today.  
    For example: {Work-order-1} is S2-40001.
  - Starting screen: Reports > Sales By Representative is open for today in a desktop browser at least 1024 px wide.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

**New preconditions**

  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Have two active staff members at the test location with their "Sales Representative" switch on: sales rep A and sales rep B.
  - Use a customer with no Sales Representative assigned.
  - Make sure the test location's Shop Supplies Charge is 0% and nothing else is invoiced at the test location today.
  - Have a work order for that customer with one labor line of 2.00 hours at $100.00 an hour and one part with cost $30.00 sold at $50.00, Labor set to sales rep A and Parts set to sales rep B, and invoice it today.
  - Open Reports > Sales By Representative in a desktop browser at least 1024 pixels wide, with the date range set to today.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

**Old steps**

  1. Expand {Sales-rep-B}.
  2. Expand {Sales-rep-A}.

**New steps**

  1. Expand sales rep B.
  2. Expand sales rep A.

**Old results**

  - Step 1:
    - {Work-order-1}'s Parts row under {Sales-rep-B}: Parts Margin $20.00 and Margin % 40.00% (20 ÷ 50 × 100); labor plays no part.
  - Step 2:
    - {Work-order-1}'s Labor row under {Sales-rep-A}: Margin % equals Labor Margin ÷ $200.00 × 100, from the labor figures only.

**New results**

  - Step 1:
    - The work order's Parts row under sales rep B shows Parts Margin $20.00 and Margin % 40.00% (20 ÷ 50 × 100).
    - Labor plays no part.
  - Step 2:
    - The work order's Labor row under sales rep A shows a Margin % equal to Labor Margin ÷ $200.00 × 100, from the labor figures only.

### S5-12 — C665973 (https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/665973)

Below the results byte-identical: quotes yes, design block yes, notes yes, AUTOMATION line yes; other fields unchanged. Facts checked 11, not found 0.

**Old preconditions**

  - Scenario: A work order with different Labor and Parts representatives was invoiced today, and you export the Expanded report with the Sales Type column off.
  - You are signed in as a user with the "Admin" role at {Location-A}.  
    For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919.
  - Two active staff members at {Location-A} have their "Sales Representative" switch on: {Sales-rep-A} and {Sales-rep-B}.  
    For example: {Sales-rep-A} is ZZAUTOTEST Ann Able and {Sales-rep-B} is ZZAUTOTEST Ben Able.
  - Customer {Customer-A} has no sales representative.  
    For example: {Customer-A} is ZZAUTOTEST SSR Fleet.
  - Work order {Work-order-1} for {Customer-A} exists; it was invoiced today with a labor line and a part; Labor {Sales-rep-A}, Parts {Sales-rep-B}.  
    For example: {Work-order-1} is S2-40001.
  - Starting screen: Reports > Sales By Representative is open for today in a desktop browser at least 1024 px wide, with the Sales Type column off on screen.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

**New preconditions**

  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Have two active staff members at the test location with their "Sales Representative" switch on, so that a work order can have a different Labor and Parts representative.
  - Use a customer with no Sales Representative assigned.
  - Have a work order for that customer with a labor line and a part, Labor set to one of them and Parts set to the other, and invoice it today.
  - Open Reports > Sales By Representative in a desktop browser at least 1024 pixels wide, with the date range set to today and the Sales Type column off on screen.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

  2. Open the CSV and find the rows for {Work-order-1}'s invoice.
  3. Export the Expanded PDF and find the same rows.

**New steps**

  2. Open the CSV and find the rows for the work order's invoice.
  3. Export the Expanded PDF and find the same rows.

**Old results**

  - Step 1:
    - the CSV downloads.
  - Step 2:

  - Step 3:
    - the PDF has the Sales Type column in the same place, right after Status and before any Location column; the cells of the side a row is not credited with show a dash.

**New results**

  - Step 2:
    - It has a Sales Type column right after Status, and before Location when the file has a Location column.
    - The Labor row reads "Labor" with blank parts cells.
    - The Parts row reads "Parts" with blank labor, hours and Shop Supplies cells.
  - Step 3:
    - The PDF has the Sales Type column in the same place, right after Status and before any Location column.
    - The cells of the side a row is not credited with show a dash.

### S5-13 — C665974 (https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/665974)

Below the results byte-identical: quotes yes, design block yes, notes yes, AUTOMATION line yes; other fields unchanged. Facts checked 12, not found 0.

  - Scenario: Two work orders invoiced today have a side, or a whole invoice, worth nothing.

    For example: {Sales-rep-A} is ZZAUTOTEST Ann Able and {Sales-rep-B} is ZZAUTOTEST Ben Able.
  - Customer {Customer-A} has no sales representative.  
    For example: {Customer-A} is ZZAUTOTEST SSR Fleet.
  - {Location-A}'s Shop Supplies Charge is 0%, and nothing else is invoiced at {Location-A} today.
  - Work order {Work-order-1} for {Customer-A} has one labor line of 1.00 hour at $100.00 and no parts, with Labor {Sales-rep-A} and Parts {Sales-rep-B}; work order {Work-order-2} totals $0.00 (one labor line at $0.00) with its one Sales Representative {Sales-rep-A}. Both were invoiced today.  
    For example: {Work-order-1} is S2-40001 and {Work-order-2} is S2-40002.
  - Starting screen: Reports > Sales By Representative is open for today in a desktop browser at least 1024 px wide, with the Sales Type column turned on.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

**New preconditions**

  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Have two active staff members at the test location with their "Sales Representative" switch on: sales rep A and sales rep B.
  - Use a customer with no Sales Representative assigned.
  - Make sure the test location's Shop Supplies Charge is 0% and nothing else is invoiced at the test location today.
  - Have a first work order for that customer with one labor line of 1.00 hour at $100.00 and no parts, Labor set to sales rep A and Parts set to sales rep B.
  - Have a second work order for that customer that totals $0.00 (one labor line at $0.00), with its one Sales Representative set to sales rep A.
  - Invoice both work orders today.
  - Open Reports > Sales By Representative in a desktop browser at least 1024 pixels wide, with the date range set to today and the Sales Type column turned on.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

**Old steps**

  1. Expand {Sales-rep-B}.
  2. Expand {Sales-rep-A}.

**New steps**

  1. Expand sales rep B.
  2. Expand sales rep A.

**Old results**

  - Step 1:
    - {Sales-rep-B} has a "Parts" row of $0.00 for {Work-order-1}, and {Sales-rep-B}'s summary row counts that invoice once.
  - Step 2:
    - {Sales-rep-A} has a $0.00 row for {Work-order-2}, counted once.

**New results**

  - Step 1:
    - Sales rep B has a "Parts" row of $0.00 for the first work order.
    - Sales rep B's summary row counts that invoice once.
  - Step 2:
    - Sales rep A has a $0.00 row for the second work order, counted once.

  - Scenario: A work order with different Labor and Parts representatives was invoiced today, and the Parts representative's last name has changed since.

    For example: {Sales-rep-A} is ZZAUTOTEST Ann Able and {Sales-rep-B} is ZZAUTOTEST Ben Able.

    For example: {Customer-A} is ZZAUTOTEST SSR Fleet.

  - After that invoice, change sales rep B's last name in Settings > Staff and save.

  - Step 2:
    - the invoiced work order still shows the representatives captured when it was invoiced.

**New results**

  - Step 1:
    - Sales rep B is listed under the new name.
    - The invoice's Parts row is under it, in one row group (not split into an old-name and a new-name group).
  - Step 2:
    - The invoiced work order still shows the representatives captured when it was invoiced.

    For example: {Sales-rep-A} is ZZAUTOTEST Ann Able and {Sales-rep-C} is ZZAUTOTEST Cara Able.
  - Customer {Customer-A} has no sales representative.  
    For example: {Customer-A} is ZZAUTOTEST SSR Fleet.
  - Work order {Work-order-1} for {Customer-A} has one "Sales Representative" field reading {Sales-rep-A}; work order {Work-order-2} for {Customer-A} has one "Sales Representative" field holding {Sales-rep-C}, picked before their switch was turned off.  
    For example: {Work-order-1} is S2-40001 and {Work-order-2} is S2-40002.

**New preconditions**

  - Have a first work order for that customer with one "Sales Representative" field set to sales rep A.
  - Have a second work order for that customer with one "Sales Representative" field set to sales rep C.
  - Then turn sales rep C's "Sales Representative" switch off and save (the staff account stays active).
  - Open the first work order in a desktop browser at least 1024 pixels wide.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

  2. Click Print Work Order and read the Sales Representative row of the printout.

  4. Open the work order's menu.
  5. Click Print Work Order and read the Sales Representative row of the printout.

**New steps**

  1. Open the work order's menu.
  2. Click Print Work Order and read the Sales Representative row of the printout.
  3. Open the second work order.
  4. Open the work order's menu.
  5. Click Print Work Order and read the Sales Representative row of the printout.

**Old results**

  - Step 1:
    - the work order's menu opens.
  - Step 2:
    - the printout opens and the row reads {Sales-rep-A} (one name).
  - Step 3:
    - {Work-order-2} opens.
  - Step 4:
    - the work order's menu opens.
  - Step 5:
    - the printout opens and the row reads "{Sales-rep-C} (Inactive)".

**New results**

  - Step 1:
    - The work order's menu opens.
  - Step 2:
    - The printout opens.
    - The row reads sales rep A (one name).
  - Step 3:
    - The second work order opens.
  - Step 4:
    - The work order's menu opens.
  - Step 5:
    - The printout opens.
    - The row reads sales rep C's name followed by "(Inactive)".

  - You are signed in as a user with the "Admin" role at {Location-A}.  
    For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919.
  - Two active staff members at {Location-A} have their "Sales Representative" switch on: {Sales-rep-A} and {Sales-rep-B}.  
    For example: {Sales-rep-A} is ZZAUTOTEST Ann Able and {Sales-rep-B} is ZZAUTOTEST Ben Able.
  - Customer {Customer-A} has no sales representative.  
    For example: {Customer-A} is ZZAUTOTEST SSR Fleet.
  - Work order {Work-order-1} for {Customer-A} is open and not invoiced; it has one approved labor line and one part, and Labor reads {Sales-rep-A} and Parts held {Sales-rep-B}, whose staff record no longer exists.  
    For example: {Work-order-1} is S2-40001.
  - Starting screen: {Work-order-1} is open in a desktop browser at least 1024 px wide.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

**New preconditions**

  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Create an active staff member for this test at the test location with the "Sales Representative" switch on: sales rep A.
  - Use a customer with no Sales Representative assigned.
  - Have an open, uninvoiced work order for that customer, with one approved labor line, one part, Labor set to sales rep A and Parts holding a staff member made for this test whose staff record no longer exists.
  - Open the work order in a desktop browser at least 1024 pixels wide.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

**Old steps**

  1. Open the work order's menu.
  2. Click Print Work Order and read the Sales Representative row of the printout.

**New steps**

  1. Open the work order's menu.
  2. Click Print Work Order and read the Sales Representative row of the printout.

**Old results**

  - Step 1:
    - the work order's menu opens.
  - Step 2:
    - the row reads "Unassigned (Parts) · {Sales-rep-A} (Labor)"; it never reads a bare "(Inactive)".

**New results**

  - Step 1:
    - The work order's menu opens.
  - Step 2:
    - The row reads "Unassigned (Parts) · [sales rep A's name] (Labor)".
    - It never reads a bare "(Inactive)".

### S5-16 — C665992 (https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/665992)

Title: Line fees stay with their side and a processing fee is shared by size

Below the results byte-identical: quotes yes, design block yes, notes yes, AUTOMATION line yes; other fields unchanged. Facts checked 30, not found 0.

**Old preconditions**

  - Scenario: A work order with different Labor and Parts representatives has a fee and a discount on its lines and a fee on the whole invoice.
  - You are signed in as a user with the "Admin" role at {Location-A}.  
    For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919.
  - Two active staff members at {Location-A} have their "Sales Representative" switch on: {Sales-rep-A} and {Sales-rep-B}.  
    For example: {Sales-rep-A} is ZZAUTOTEST Ann Able and {Sales-rep-B} is ZZAUTOTEST Ben Able.
  - Customer {Customer-A} has no sales representative.  
    For example: {Customer-A} is ZZAUTOTEST SSR Fleet.
  - {Location-A}'s Shop Supplies Charge is 0%, and nothing else is invoiced at {Location-A} today.
  - Work order {Work-order-1} for {Customer-A} is open and not invoiced; it has one labor line of 3.00 hours at $100.00 ($300.00) with a labor-line discount of $30.00, one part sold at $100.00 with a part-line fee of $10.00, and a whole-invoice fee of $38.00; Labor reads {Sales-rep-A} and Parts reads {Sales-rep-B}.  
    For example: {Work-order-1} is S2-40001.
  - Starting screen: {Work-order-1} is open in a desktop browser at least 1024 px wide.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

**New preconditions**

  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Have two active staff members at the test location with their "Sales Representative" switch on: sales rep A and sales rep B.
  - Use a customer with no Sales Representative assigned.
  - Make sure the test location's Shop Supplies Charge is 0% and nothing else is invoiced at the test location today.
  - Create an open, uninvoiced work order for this test for that customer with one labor line of 3.00 hours at $100.00 ($300.00) with a labor-line discount of $30.00, one part sold at $100.00 with a part-line fee of $10.00, and a whole-invoice fee of $38.00, with Labor set to sales rep A and Parts set to sales rep B.
  - Open the work order in a desktop browser at least 1024 pixels wide.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

**Old steps**

  1. Invoice {Work-order-1}.
  2. Open Reports > Sales By Representative.
  3. Set the date range to today.
  4. Expand {Sales-rep-A}.
  5. Expand {Sales-rep-B}.

**New steps**

  1. Invoice the work order.
  2. Open Reports > Sales By Representative.
  3. Set the date range to today.
  4. Expand sales rep A.
  5. Expand sales rep B.

**Old results**

  - Step 1:
    - the invoice total before tax is $418.00 (300.00 − 30.00 + 100.00 + 10.00 + 38.00).
  - Step 2:
    - the Sales By Representative report opens.
  - Step 3:
    - the report shows today's date range.
  - Step 4:
    - {Work-order-1}'s invoice has one row under {Sales-rep-A}, its Labor row: labor $270.00 after its own discount, and its share of the whole-invoice fee in its Adjustments column, $27.00 (270 ÷ 380 × 38); subtotal $297.00.
  - Step 5:
    - its Parts row under {Sales-rep-B}: parts $110.00 after its own fee, and its share of the whole-invoice fee in its Adjustments column, $11.00 (110 ÷ 380 × 38); subtotal $121.00. The two subtotals add up to the invoice total before tax: $297.00 + $121.00 = $418.00.

**New results**

  - Step 1:
    - The invoice total before tax is $418.00 (300.00 − 30.00 + 100.00 + 10.00 + 38.00).
  - Step 2:
    - The Sales By Representative report opens.
  - Step 3:
    - The report shows today's date range.
  - Step 4:
    - The work order's invoice has one row under sales rep A, its Labor row.
    - That row shows labor $270.00 after its own discount.
    - Its Adjustments column holds its share of the whole-invoice fee, $27.00 (270 ÷ 380 × 38).
    - Its subtotal is $297.00.
  - Step 5:
    - Its Parts row under sales rep B shows parts $110.00 after its own fee.
    - Its Adjustments column holds its share of the whole-invoice fee, $11.00 (110 ÷ 380 × 38).
    - Its subtotal is $121.00.
    - The two subtotals add up to the invoice total before tax: $297.00 + $121.00 = $418.00.

Adding up by hand every invoice row's Labor Invoiced, Parts Invoiced, Shop Supplies and Hrs Invoiced: each representative's summary row equals the sum of its invoice rows, and the report totals equal the sum of all invoice rows, to the cent (hours to two decimals).

Below the results byte-identical: quotes yes, design block yes, notes yes, AUTOMATION line yes; other fields unchanged. Facts checked 7, not found 0.

**Old preconditions**

  - Scenario: A customer had a split work order invoiced and a credit memo made today.
  - You are signed in as a user with the "Admin" role at {Location-A}.  
    For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919.
  - Two active staff members at {Location-A} have their "Sales Representative" switch on: {Sales-rep-A} and {Sales-rep-B}.  
    For example: {Sales-rep-A} is ZZAUTOTEST Ann Able and {Sales-rep-B} is ZZAUTOTEST Ben Able.
  - Customer {Customer-A} has no sales representative.  
    For example: {Customer-A} is ZZAUTOTEST SSR Fleet.
  - Today {Customer-A} has an invoiced split work order (Labor {Sales-rep-A}, Parts {Sales-rep-B}) and a credit memo of $20.00.
  - Starting screen: Reports > Sales By Representative is open for today in a desktop browser at least 1024 px wide, with Show Unassigned on.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

**New preconditions**

  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Have two active staff members at the test location with their "Sales Representative" switch on: sales rep A and sales rep B.
  - Use a customer with no Sales Representative assigned.
  - Have a split work order for that customer (Labor set to sales rep A, Parts set to sales rep B), invoiced today, and a credit memo of $20.00 for that customer today.
  - Open Reports > Sales By Representative in a desktop browser at least 1024 pixels wide, with the date range set to today and Show Unassigned on.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

**Old steps**

  1. Click the chevron in the Date column header to expand every row.

**New steps**

  1. Click the chevron in the Date column header to expand every row.

**Old results**

  - Step 1:
    - for {Customer-A}, the work order's Labor row shows under {Sales-rep-A} and its Parts row under {Sales-rep-B}; the $20.00 credit memo is not in the report.

**New results**

  - Step 1:
    - For the customer, the work order's Labor row shows under sales rep A and its Parts row under sales rep B.
    - The $20.00 credit memo is not in the report.

### REL-11 — C666013 (https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/666013)

Title: No sales representative is sent to QuickBooks

Below the results byte-identical: quotes yes, design block yes, notes yes, AUTOMATION line yes; other fields unchanged. Facts checked 4, not found 0.

**Old preconditions**

  - Scenario: Your location is connected to QuickBooks, and you invoice a work order with different Labor and Parts representatives.
  - You are signed in as a user with the "Admin" role at {Location-A}.  
    For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919.
  - Two active staff members at {Location-A} have their "Sales Representative" switch on: {Sales-rep-A} and {Sales-rep-B}.  
    For example: {Sales-rep-A} is ZZAUTOTEST Ann Able and {Sales-rep-B} is ZZAUTOTEST Ben Able.
  - Customer {Customer-A} has no sales representative.  
    For example: {Customer-A} is ZZAUTOTEST SSR Fleet.
  - {Location-A} is connected to a QuickBooks test company.
  - Work order {Work-order-1} for {Customer-A} is open and not invoiced; it has one approved labor line and one part, and Labor reads {Sales-rep-A} and Parts reads {Sales-rep-B}.  
    For example: {Work-order-1} is S2-40001.
  - Starting screen: {Work-order-1} is open in a desktop browser at least 1024 px wide.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

**New preconditions**

  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Have two active staff members at the test location with their "Sales Representative" switch on, so that a work order can have a different Labor and Parts representative.
  - Use a customer with no Sales Representative assigned.
  - Make sure the test location is connected to a QuickBooks test company.
  - Create an open, uninvoiced work order for this test for that customer, with one approved labor line, one part and "Sales Representative - Labor" set to one of them and "Sales Representative - Parts" set to the other.
  - Open the work order in a desktop browser at least 1024 pixels wide.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

**Old steps**

  1. Invoice {Work-order-1}.
  2. Wait for the invoice to sync to QuickBooks.
  3. Open the invoice in QuickBooks.

**New steps**

  1. Invoice the work order.
  2. Wait for the invoice to sync to QuickBooks.
  3. Open the invoice in QuickBooks.

**Old results**

  - Step 1:
    - the invoice is created.
  - Step 2:
    - the invoice syncs.
  - Step 3:
    - no sales representative appears on the QuickBooks invoice.

**New results**

  - Step 1:
    - The invoice is created.
  - Step 2:
    - The invoice syncs.
  - Step 3:
    - No sales representative appears on the QuickBooks invoice.

### S2-14 — C690667 (https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/690667)

  - Step 4:

  - Step 5:

  - Step 6:
    - once its save finishes, the second window shows what was saved: one "Sales Representative" field reading {Sales-rep-B}.
  - Step 7:

  - Step 8:
    - the second window still shows one "Sales Representative" field reading {Sales-rep-B}.

Reading it from top to bottom: "Unassigned" is first, then every active sales representative sorted by first name, then last name, A to Z (including {Sales-rep-A} and {Sales-rep-B}); {Staff-member-D} is not listed; there is no search box.

### S4-26 — C690669 (https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/690669)

Title: Reactivating a rep does not put them back on their customers

Below the results byte-identical: quotes yes, design block yes, notes yes, AUTOMATION line yes; other fields unchanged. Facts checked 13, not found 0.

**Old preconditions**

  - Scenario: A sales representative was on two customers before their staff account was deactivated, and now the account is reactivated.
  - You are signed in as a user with the "Admin" role at {Location-A}.  
    For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919.
  - Staff members {Sales-rep-A} and {Sales-rep-B} are active at {Location-A} with the "Sales Representative" switch on. Staff member {Sales-rep-C} had the switch on, and their staff account has since been deactivated.  
    For example: {Sales-rep-A} is ZZAUTOTEST Ann Able, {Sales-rep-B} is ZZAUTOTEST Ben Able and {Sales-rep-C} is ZZAUTOTEST Cara Able.
  - Before {Sales-rep-C} was deactivated, customer {Customer-A} had {Sales-rep-C} as its one sales representative and customer {Customer-B} showed "Sales Representative - Service Labor" {Sales-rep-A}, "Sales Representative - Service Parts" {Sales-rep-B} and "Sales Representative - Part Sales" {Sales-rep-C}.  
    For example: {Customer-A} is ZZAUTOTEST SSR Fleet and {Customer-B} is ZZAUTOTEST SSR Other.
  - Starting screen: {Sales-rep-C}'s staff record is open in Settings > Staff in a desktop browser at least 1024 px wide.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

**New preconditions**

  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Create three active staff members for this test at the test location with their "Sales Representative" switch on: sales rep A, sales rep B and sales rep C.
  - Create customer A for this test, whose one Sales Representative is sales rep C.
  - Create customer B for this test, with "Sales Representative - Service Labor" set to sales rep A, "Sales Representative - Service Parts" set to sales rep B and "Sales Representative - Part Sales" set to sales rep C.
  - Then deactivate sales rep C's staff account.
  - Open sales rep C's staff record in Settings > Staff in a desktop browser at least 1024 pixels wide.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

**Old steps**

  1. Reactivate {Sales-rep-C}'s account.
  2. Open {Customer-A}'s page.
  3. Open {Customer-B}'s page.

**New steps**

  1. Reactivate sales rep C's account.
  2. Open customer A's page.
  3. Open customer B's page.

**Old results**

  - Step 1:
    - {Sales-rep-C}'s account is active again.
  - Step 2:
    - {Customer-A}'s "Sales Representative" reads "Unassigned"; {Sales-rep-C} has not been put back.
  - Step 3:
    - {Customer-B} shows "Sales Representative - Service Labor" {Sales-rep-A} and "Sales Representative - Service Parts" {Sales-rep-B}, with no Part Sales field; {Sales-rep-C} has not been put back.

**New results**

  - Step 1:
    - Sales rep C's account is active again.
  - Step 2:
    - Customer A's "Sales Representative" reads "Unassigned".
    - Sales rep C has not been put back.
  - Step 3:
    - Customer B shows "Sales Representative - Service Labor" sales rep A and "Sales Representative - Service Parts" sales rep B.
    - There is no Part Sales field.
    - Sales rep C has not been put back.

    For example: {Customer-A} is ZZAUTOTEST SSR Fleet.
  - Work order {Work-order-1} for {Customer-A} is open and not invoiced; "Sales Representative - Labor" reads {Sales-rep-A} and "Sales Representative - Parts" holds {Staff-member-C}, whose staff record no longer exists.  
    For example: {Work-order-1} is S2-40001 and {Staff-member-C} is ZZAUTOTEST Gone Rep.
  - Work order {Work-order-2} for {Customer-A} is open and not invoiced; its one "Sales Representative" field holds {Staff-member-D}, a staff member of another organization.

  - Create two active staff members for this test at the test location with their "Sales Representative" switch on: sales rep A and sales rep B.
  - Use a customer with no Sales Representative assigned.
  - Create a first work order for this test for that customer, open and not invoiced, whose "Sales Representative - Labor" field reads sales rep A and whose "Sales Representative - Parts" field holds a staff member made for this test whose staff record no longer exists.
  - Create a second work order for this test for that customer, open and not invoiced, whose one "Sales Representative" field holds a staff member made for this test in another organization.
  - Open the first work order in a desktop browser at least 1024 pixels wide.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

  2. Open the work order's menu.

  4. Close the log.
  5. Open {Work-order-2}.
  6. Set its Sales Representative to {Sales-rep-A}.
  7. Open the work order's menu.
  8. Click Audit Log.

**New steps**

  2. Open the work order's menu.
  3. Click Audit Log.
  4. Close the log.
  5. Open the second work order.
  6. Set its Sales Representative to sales rep A.
  7. Open the work order's menu.
  8. Click Audit Log.

    - Labor reads {Sales-rep-B}.
  - Step 2:
    - the work order's menu opens.
  - Step 3:
    - the newest entry reads "Sales Representative: Unassigned (Parts) · {Sales-rep-A} (Labor) → Unassigned (Parts) · {Sales-rep-B} (Labor)"; the Parts side never reads a bare "(Inactive)".
  - Step 4:
    - the log closes.
  - Step 5:
    - {Work-order-2} opens.
  - Step 6:
    - the field reads {Sales-rep-A}.
  - Step 7:
    - the work order's menu opens.
  - Step 8:
    - the newest entry reads "Sales Representative: Unassigned → {Sales-rep-A}".

**New results**

  - Step 1:
    - Labor reads sales rep B.
  - Step 2:
    - The work order's menu opens.
  - Step 3:
    - The newest entry reads "Sales Representative: Unassigned (Parts) · [sales rep A's name] (Labor) → Unassigned (Parts) · [sales rep B's name] (Labor)".
    - The Parts side never reads a bare "(Inactive)".
  - Step 4:
    - The log closes.
  - Step 5:
    - The second work order opens.
  - Step 6:
    - The field reads sales rep A.
  - Step 7:
    - The work order's menu opens.
  - Step 8:
    - The newest entry reads "Sales Representative: Unassigned → [sales rep A's name]".

### S5-20 — C715569 (https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/715569)

Title: A discount bigger than the invoice is shared by size and both rows end at $0.00

Below the results byte-identical: quotes yes, design block yes, notes yes, AUTOMATION line yes; other fields unchanged. Facts checked 22, not found 0.

**Old preconditions**

  - Scenario: A work order for a tax-exempt customer, with different Labor and Parts representatives, has a discount bigger than the invoice.
  - You are signed in as a user with the "Admin" role at {Location-A}.  
    For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919.
  - Two active staff members at {Location-A} have their "Sales Representative" switch on: {Sales-rep-A} and {Sales-rep-B}.  
    For example: {Sales-rep-A} is ZZAUTOTEST Ann Able and {Sales-rep-B} is ZZAUTOTEST Ben Able.
  - Customer {Customer-A} has no sales representative, and no tax applies to its invoices.  
    For example: {Customer-A} is ZZAUTOTEST SSR Tax Exempt, set as tax exempt.
  - {Location-A}'s Shop Supplies Charge is 0%, and nothing else is invoiced at {Location-A} today.
  - Work order {Work-order-1} for {Customer-A} is open and not invoiced; it has one labor line of 1.00 hour at $100.00 ($100.00), one part sold at $50.00 and a non-taxable whole-invoice discount of $200.00 (not on any line); Labor reads {Sales-rep-A} and Parts reads {Sales-rep-B}.  
    For example: {Work-order-1} is S2-40001.
  - Starting screen: {Work-order-1} is open in a desktop browser at least 1024 px wide.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

**New preconditions**

  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Have two active staff members at the test location with their "Sales Representative" switch on: sales rep A and sales rep B.
  - Use a customer with no Sales Representative assigned, set as tax exempt so that no tax applies to its invoices.
  - Make sure the test location's Shop Supplies Charge is 0% and nothing else is invoiced at the test location today.
  - Create an open, uninvoiced work order for this test for that customer with one labor line of 1.00 hour at $100.00 ($100.00), one part sold at $50.00 and a non-taxable whole-invoice discount of $200.00 (not on any line), with Labor set to sales rep A and Parts set to sales rep B.
  - Open the work order in a desktop browser at least 1024 pixels wide.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

**Old steps**

  1. Invoice {Work-order-1}.
  2. Open Reports > Sales By Representative.
  3. Set the date range to today.
  4. Expand {Sales-rep-A}.
  5. Expand {Sales-rep-B}.

**New steps**

  1. Invoice the work order.
  2. Open Reports > Sales By Representative.
  3. Set the date range to today.
  4. Expand sales rep A.
  5. Expand sales rep B.

**Old results**

  - Step 1:
    - the invoice total is $0.00: the discount does not take it below zero (100.00 + 50.00 − 200.00 would be −50.00, so $50.00 is added back).
  - Step 2:
    - the Sales By Representative report opens.
  - Step 3:
    - the report shows today's date range.
  - Step 4:
    - {Work-order-1}'s invoice has one row under {Sales-rep-A}, its Labor row: Labor Invoiced $100.00, Adjustments ($100.00), its share of the −$150.00 net whole-invoice amount (100 ÷ 150 × 150), subtotal $0.00.
  - Step 5:
    - its Parts row under {Sales-rep-B}: Parts Invoiced $50.00, Adjustments ($50.00) (50 ÷ 150 × 150), subtotal $0.00. $0.00 + $0.00 = $0.00, the invoice total; neither row goes below zero and the report total for the invoice is $0.00.

**New results**

  - Step 1:
    - The invoice total is $0.00.
    - The discount does not take it below zero (100.00 + 50.00 − 200.00 would be −50.00, so $50.00 is added back).
  - Step 2:
    - The Sales By Representative report opens.
  - Step 3:
    - The report shows today's date range.
  - Step 4:
    - The work order's invoice has one row under sales rep A, its Labor row.
    - That row shows Labor Invoiced $100.00.
    - Its Adjustments reads ($100.00), its share of the −$150.00 net whole-invoice amount (100 ÷ 150 × 150).
    - Its subtotal is $0.00.
  - Step 5:
    - Its Parts row under sales rep B shows Parts Invoiced $50.00.
    - Its Adjustments reads ($50.00) (50 ÷ 150 × 150).
    - Its subtotal is $0.00.
    - $0.00 + $0.00 = $0.00, the invoice total.
    - Neither row goes below zero.
    - The report total for the invoice is $0.00.

    - the filter opens again.
  - Step 5:
    - two options are ticked.

    - the label reads the count and the filter's own word, as before the release (for example "2 locations"); it does not list the two option names the way the new Sales Type filter does.

**New results**

    - The filter opens with its options.
  - Step 2:
    - Every option is ticked.
  - Step 3:
    - The label reads "All" followed by the filter's own word, as before the release (for example "All locations").
  - Step 4:
    - The filter opens again.
  - Step 5:
    - Two options are ticked.
  - Step 6:
    - The label reads the count and the filter's own word, as before the release (for example "2 locations").
    - It does not list the two option names the way the new Sales Type filter does.

Below the results byte-identical: quotes yes, design block yes, notes yes, AUTOMATION line yes; other fields unchanged. Facts checked 7, not found 0.

    For example: {Sales-rep-A} is ZZAUTOTEST Ann Able and {Sales-rep-B} is ZZAUTOTEST Ben Able.
  - Customer {Customer-A} has no sales representative.  
    For example: {Customer-A} is ZZAUTOTEST SSR Fleet.
  - Before the release was installed, work order {Work-order-1} for {Customer-A} had its Sales Representative changed from {Sales-rep-A} to {Sales-rep-B}, which wrote a "Sales representative changed" entry, and a version of it was saved to its history with {Sales-rep-B}; it is still open and not invoiced.  
    For example: {Work-order-1} is S2-39002.

  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

**New preconditions**

  - Have two active staff members at the test location with their "Sales Representative" switch on: sales rep A and sales rep B.
  - Use a customer with no Sales Representative assigned.
  - Use a work order for that customer, still open and not invoiced, whose Sales Representative was changed from sales rep A to sales rep B before the release was installed. That change wrote a "Sales representative changed" entry, and a version of the work order was saved to its history with sales rep B.
  - With the release installed, open the work order in a desktop browser at least 1024 pixels wide.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

**New steps**

**Old results**

  - Step 1:
    - the work order's menu opens.
  - Step 2:
    - the entry still reads "Sales Representative: {Sales-rep-A} → {Sales-rep-B}", one name on each side of the arrow, with no (Parts) or (Labor).

**New results**

  - Step 1:
    - The work order's menu opens.
  - Step 2:
    - The entry still reads "Sales Representative: [sales rep A's name] → [sales rep B's name]".
    - There is one name on each side of the arrow, with no (Parts) or (Labor).

    - The change made while the first was saving is ignored.

**New results**

    - The dropdown opens.
  - Step 2:
    - The change is saved, not refused.
    - Parts reads sales rep A.
    - Labor still reads sales rep C's name followed by "(Inactive)".
    - No error message shows.

### S4-03b — C724219 (https://shopview.testrail.io/index.php?/cases/view/724219)

Title: Adding Service Parts starts Unassigned when the rep's switch is off

Below the results byte-identical: quotes yes, design block yes, notes yes, AUTOMATION line yes; other fields unchanged. Facts checked 11, not found 0.

**Old preconditions**

  - Scenario: A customer's one representative has had their switch turned off, and you add a separate service parts representative.
  - You are signed in as a user with the "Admin" role at {Location-A}.  
    For example: {Location-A} is Staging Heavy Duty - 9919.
  - Staff member {Staff-member-D} is active at {Location-A} with the "Sales Representative" switch turned off after being picked on the customer.  
    For example: {Staff-member-D} is ZZAUTOTEST Dan Noswitch.
  - Customer {Customer-A} has {Staff-member-D} as its one sales representative, shown as "{Staff-member-D} (Inactive)".  
    For example: {Customer-A} is ZZAUTOTEST SSR Switch Off.
  - Starting screen: {Customer-A}'s Edit Customer window is open in a desktop browser at least 1024 px wide.
  - If any record above is missing, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

**New preconditions**

  - Sign in as an Admin at the test location.
  - Create staff member D for this test, active at the test location with the "Sales Representative" switch on.
  - Create a customer for this test whose one Sales Representative is staff member D.
  - Then turn staff member D's "Sales Representative" switch off and save, so the customer shows staff member D's name followed by "(Inactive)".
  - Open the customer's Edit Customer window in a desktop browser at least 1024 pixels wide.
  - If you cannot set up any of the above, mark this test Blocked: "setup not done".

**Old steps**

  1. Click the + beside Sales Representative.
  2. Click "Sales Representative - Service Parts".
  3. Save the customer.

**New steps**

  1. Click the + beside Sales Representative.
  2. Click "Sales Representative - Service Parts".
  3. Save the customer.

**Old results**

  - Step 1:
    - the menu opens.
  - Step 2:
    - the first field is renamed "Sales Representative - Service Labor" and still reads "{Staff-member-D} (Inactive)"; "Sales Representative - Service Parts" shows under it reading "Unassigned".
  - Step 3:
    - the customer saves, and its page lists "Sales Representative - Service Labor" {Staff-member-D} (Inactive) and "Sales Representative - Service Parts" Unassigned.

**New results**

  - Step 1:
    - The menu opens.
  - Step 2:
    - The first field is renamed "Sales Representative - Service Labor".
    - It still reads staff member D's name followed by "(Inactive)".
    - "Sales Representative - Service Parts" shows under it reading "Unassigned".
  - Step 3:
    - The customer saves.
    - Its page lists "Sales Representative - Service Labor" with staff member D's name followed by "(Inactive)".
    - Its page lists "Sales Representative - Service Parts" with Unassigned.
